### [1] [Direct From Darwin: Deriving Advanced Optimizers From Evolutionary First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05284)
*Daniel Grimmer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文从进化第一性原理出发推导出一系列梯度优化算法，证明Fisher和Wright的进化观点在无性繁殖背景下形式等价，并通过引入DLS噪声关系，表明SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法乃至Adam等现代优化算法均可成为达尔文进化的科学有效模拟工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化计算长期以来承诺同时提供高性能优化工具和达尔文进化的科学模拟，但现代算法常偏离进化保真度，采用物理学启发式或浅层生物学隐喻。本文旨在弥合这一差距，从进化第一性原理统一优化与进化模拟。

Method: 提出达尔文世系模拟（DLS）框架，证明Fisher确定性进化的总群体与Wright随机漂变的子群体形式等价；引入DLS噪声关系作为结构化噪声，证明任何满足该关系的簿记选择都能忠实模拟进化；利用这一表示自由度，将SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法和Adam等优化算法纳入进化兼容框架。

Result: 证明了Fisher和Wright的进化观点在无性繁殖背景下形式等价；展示了SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法等优化算法通过添加DLS噪声即可成为进化上有效的模拟；Adam优化器通过轻微数学调整也可符合进化原则。

Conclusion: 优化算法与进化模拟并非互斥，通过恰当的结构化噪声（遗传漂变）设计，现代优化算法可以同时是高性能优化工具和科学有效的达尔文进化模拟。

Abstract: Evolutionary computation has long promised to deliver both high-performance optimization tools as well as rigorous scientific simulations of Darwinian evolution. However, modern algorithms frequently abandon evolutionary fidelity for physics-inspired heuristics or superficial biological metaphors. This paper derives a suite of advanced gradient-based optimization algorithms directly from evolutionary first principles. We introduce Darwinian Lineage Simulations (DLS) to prove that, in an asexual context, Fisher's and Wright's historically opposed views of evolution are actually formally equivalent. This unification requires carefully partitioning Fisher's deterministically-evolving total population into Wright's randomly-drifting sub-populations. We prove that proper bookkeeping requires introducing a specific kind of structured noise (the DLS noise relation). Crucially, however, any bookkeeping choices which satisfy this relation will result in a faithful simulation of evolution. Using this vast representational freedom, we prove that a broad family of battle-tested optimization algorithms are already perfectly compatible with evolutionary dynamics. These include: Stochastic Gradient Descent, Natural Gradient Descent, and the Damped Newton's method among many others. By simply adding DLS noise (i.e., evolutionarily faithful genetic drift), these algorithms become scientifically valid in silico simulations of Darwinian evolution. Finally, we demonstrate that even the state-of-the-art Adam optimizer can be brought into evolutionary compliance through a minor mathematical surgery.

</details>

### [2] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用进化策略（Evolution Strategy）优化预训练量化模型的权重分配，通过迭代微调少量权重到不同量化状态，以提升最近邻量化方法导致的精度损失。在VGG、ResNet和自编码器上的实验表明，该方法能快速提高量化模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备、实时系统等场景中受限于复杂度和内存大小，量化是主流压缩技术。但现有基于最近邻量化的方法无法保证最优精度，因此需要更高效的量化优化方法。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化方法，每次迭代中仅改变小比例权重的值，将其迁移到不同的量化状态。通过精心设计的算子与参数，在保持模型已量化结构的同时优化精度。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet（图像分类与检测）以及自编码器架构上进行了实验验证，结果表明所提出的进化方法能够快速提升量化模型的准确率。

Conclusion: 进化策略配合适当的算子与参数可以有效改进最近邻量化模型的精度，为深度模型压缩提供了一种新的优化思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [3] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计分层模型，实现了慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）的早期预测，AUC达0.8461，优于基线模型0.0168，表明常规EHR数据可用于人群代表性CRS风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRS是一种常见但异质性强的炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，症状与过敏性鼻炎等重叠，且表型异质性掩盖了风险模式。既往预测研究多依赖单一机构队列，缺乏人群层面的普适性。

Method: 利用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据，选取诊断前两年病史；采用混合特征选择管道（基于患病率的统计筛查+基于模型的重要性排序），将约11万候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征；针对六组成年人性别与生命阶段亚组训练人口统计分层模型，并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 整体AUC达0.8461，较最佳基线模型提升0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可用于人群代表性的CRS风险分层，为初级保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先级提供依据。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [4] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT)，一种无需中央协调器的多智能体训练范式，通过分块坐标更新、序列感知优势估计和每智能体KL信任域，实现小模型团队超越大模型，并保证单调改进和即插即用不变性。三个4B智能体组成的团队在AIME24/25上超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然性能强大但部署成本过高，而现有的多智能体协作方法在联合更新时面临复合分布偏移问题，导致协调困难和训练不稳定，亟需一种可扩展、去中心化的训练框架。

Method: 将团队表示为因子化策略，采用分块坐标更新实现无协调器分布式训练；提出序列感知的on-policy优势估计器，条件化于演化中的团队策略；结合每智能体KL信任域隔离占领漂移；理论上证明单调改进保证和即插即用不变性（任意智能体可升级为更强模型而无需重训练团队）。

Result: 三个4B智能体（总计12B参数）组成的团队在AIME24/25基准上超越Qwen3-32B模型平均3.9%；将其中两个智能体替换为8B模型后，复合分数进一步提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT框架成功解决了多智能体联合训练中的分布漂移问题，实现了无需中央协调器的可扩展训练，并提供了即插即用的理论保证，为低成本、高性能的LLM部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [5] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格联系，通过引入时域约束Rashomon集框架，揭示了混沌系统中模型多样性随预测时域指数演化的规律，并提出了基于李雅普诺夫指数的加权度量与决策对齐的模型选择算法，在合成和真实系统中将决策质量提升18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（不同近似最优模型间存在显著差异）与混沌动力学（初始微小误差指数放大）是机器学习中两个独立发展的基本挑战，尽管它们存在概念上的内在联系，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解混沌系统中的模型多样性演化规律。

Method: 1) 引入时域约束Rashomon集，刻画混沌系统中预测时域对模型多重性的影响；2) 证明有效Rashomon集以最大李雅普诺夫指数决定的速率随提前时间指数收缩；3) 提出李雅普诺夫加权度量，为预测分歧提供更紧的界；4) 开发决策对齐的模型选择算法，基于下游效用而非单纯预测精度进行模型选取。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等真实应用中，所提框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升18-34%。

Conclusion: 本文桥接了混沌理论与预测多重性两个独立领域，为在安全关键的混沌领域中部署机器学习模型提供了理论指导原则，即需考虑预测时域对模型等效性的根本影响，并基于下游决策效用而非单纯精度来选择模型。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [6] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了概念操控的理论框架，建立了Steering与概念擦除（LEACE）之间的联系，提出了LEACE-Switch框架，并在此基础上引入更通用的MidSteer方法，实现最小干扰的概念导向变换，在多种模型和任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Steering中间表示在控制生成模型（特别是对齐与安全）方面效果显著，但缺乏统一的数学理论框架，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 建立Steering与LEACE（仿射概念擦除）之间的理论联系；2) 提出LEACE-Switch框架，用于概念切换，并刻画其最优仿射解的条件；3) 提出MidSteer（最小干扰概念操控），一种更通用的仿射框架，放宽假设实现定向最小干扰变换。

Result: MidSteer在视觉扩散模型和大语言模型等多种任务、模态和架构中均表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文为概念操控提供了系统的理论框架，MidSteer作为一种通用方法在保持最小干扰的同时有效实现定向概念变换，具有良好的跨架构泛化能力。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [7] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种轻量级可学习逐token门控机制，通过在连续transformer块间对残差更新进行选择性路由，在仅增加1.7%参数且无需改变基础架构的情况下，实现端到端可微的难度比例动态跳层。实验表明，即使无深度正则化，任务损失梯度本身即可驱动路由器跳过20%的token-层操作，在字符级语言建模任务上以<0.5%质量损失节省14-23%计算量，并在同等效率下比early exit低0.7%验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准transformer对所有token施以相同层数，忽略了不同token的上下文难度差异，导致计算效率低下。作者希望设计一种可学习的、与架构无关的轻量级机制，让模型根据每个token的难度动态决定是否跳过某些层，从而在保持性能的同时提升计算效率。

Method: 在每个连续transformer块之间插入一个可学习的门控模块（两层MLP），对每个token的残差更新输出一个连续的暂停概率（halting probability），实现端到端可微。该机制仅增加1.7%参数，不改变base架构。门控通过任务损失梯度自然学习到难度比例路由，无需显式深度正则化（λ=0时仍可跳过20%操作）。

Result: 在字符级语言建模任务上，TSA在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8数据集上以<0.5%的质量损失节省了14-23%的token-层操作（TLOps）。在同等计算效率下，TSA比early exit方法低0.7%的验证损失。学习到的路由策略可直接迁移到推理时的稀疏执行，实现真实的 wall-clock 加速。

Conclusion: TSA是一种高效、可微、与架构无关的动态跳层方法，能够根据token的上下文难度自适应调整计算量，在几乎不损失质量的前提下显著降低计算成本，且具备良好的推理效率可迁移性。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [8] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何框架分析稀疏自编码器中特征组合的不稳定性，推导出组合坍缩阈值，并揭示ReLU整流在组合过程中产生类似棘轮效应的干扰累积，验证了结构化语义特征上的标度趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在解耦特征叠加和实现激活操控方面表现优异，但组合式操控（同时激活多个语义潜变量）的理论基础尚不清晰，现有的线性表示假说忽略了过完备字典中的非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，基于球面字典模型推导组合坍缩的渐近阈值（由信号锥的高斯平均宽度/统计维度刻画）；分析高偏置下ReLU整流如何将微观相关性诱导的方差波动转化为系统性漂移，形成棘轮效应式的干扰累积。

Result: 在CLEVR数据集的结构化语义特征上验证了预测的标度趋势，层级相关性会加速组合坍缩的过渡，相较于随机基线更早出现干扰增长。

Conclusion: 结果表明基于联合（union）的操控可扩展性受到几何约束，组合机制需要显式管理超出朴素线性叠加的干扰效应。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [9] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习样本在预训练-微调范式下的效果，发现冻结预训练权重会削弱现有UE方法的效果，并提出了一种名为浅层语义伪装（SSC）的分层欺骗策略，通过在语义有效子空间中生成扰动来保持数据的不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习样本（UEs）的研究主要评估从零训练的场景，但在广泛采用的预训练-微调（PF）范式下的行为尚未被探索。

Method: 提出浅层语义伪装（SSC）策略，将扰动生成过程限制在语义有效子空间中，以绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。

Result: SSC方法在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等具有挑战性的训练范式下，仍能一致地保持数据的不可学习性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习中的关键空白，揭示了PF范式下UEs失效的原因（语义过滤），并提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [10] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的效率瓶颈（掉队者效应），提出了一种无需训练、模态感知的容量缩放框架MACS，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制，分别解决视觉token的信息异质性和模态动态性两大挑战，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中存在严重效率瓶颈（掉队者效应），而现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法应对多模态场景下的两个独特挑战：信息异质性（大量冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待）和模态动态性（不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配）。

Method: 提出MACS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制——通过熵量化视觉token的语义价值，解决信息异质性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制——根据输入实时模态组成分配专家资源，解决模态动态性。

Result: 在多种多模态基准测试上显著优于现有方法，提供了高效部署MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中的新型鲁棒方案。

Conclusion: MACS是一种无需训练的高效推理框架，有效解决了MoE MLLMs在EP推理中的掉队者效应，为多模态场景下的负载均衡提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [11] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种监督内化方法，将强化学习中仅有的结局反馈自动转化为过程级学习信号，使模型能够通过识别、修正和复用失败推理轨迹，实现更细粒度的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习推理方法面临两难：结局级奖励难以进行精细信用分配，而外部过程监督成本高且难以可持续扩展。亟需一种能从结局监督中自动提取过程级信号的方法。

Method: 提出监督内化视角：将强化学习推理理解为把结局监督内化为过程监督的问题。具体通过识别、修正和复用失败推理轨迹，使模型自动提取过程级学习信号，实现细粒度策略优化。

Result: 建立了新的训练范式：模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并精炼自身的内部过程监督，在仅有结局监督的条件下实现了细粒度信用分配。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了一条区别于外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配新路径，为强化学习推理中的信用分配问题提供了可行且可持续的解决方案。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [12] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT是一个在线数据重加权框架，通过自适应样本学习率动态调整训练样本重要性，替代传统离线数据筛选/混合方法，在指令微调和大规模预训练中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据筛选方法采用离线范式，与训练过程脱节，导致工程开销大、模型/任务迁移时需重新运行整个流程；且通过硬筛选或重采样改变数据量，牺牲多样性，损害泛化能力。

Method: 将数据筛选重构为在线重加权问题，提出ADAPT框架：在训练过程中通过基于相似性的质量信号指导自适应样本学习率，动态调整样本重要性权重，不改变训练样本数量，实现隐式课程学习。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT在相同FLOPs下一致优于离线筛选/混合方法及现有在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 数据筛选应从静态离线处理转向动态在线重加权，ADAPT通过隐式课程学习机制有效提升LLM训练效率和泛化性能。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [13] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过逐元素评估Transformer层中参数对特定任务的重要性，并据此调整梯度更新，在持续学习中平衡旧任务保持与新任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法（数据回放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知能力，无法区分哪些参数应当保留或更新，导致灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架：为每个Transformer层计算任务特定的逐元素参数重要性分数，利用这些分数调制梯度——对先前任务重要的参数施加较小更新，而对不重要的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准上，该方法一致优于基线方法，在保持旧任务性能的同时，在新任务上取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 基于归因的参数重要性估计能够有效指导LLM持续学习中的梯度更新，实现更好的灾难性遗忘缓解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [14] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法提升7%-32%，且速度快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体通过率极低（<5%），且需要在缺乏先验数据的新型蛋白质上高效迭代筛选高质量配体，现有方法效率不足。

Method: SPADE算法通过从稀疏数据中学习，采用新颖的配体选择策略，以极少的测试次数（平均40次）找到10个高质量配体。

Result: 在一对一比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%，且评分速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，可用于新型蛋白质的早期药物发现。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [15] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练扩散模型在线性逆问题（如修复和超分辨率）中的零样本条件采样。通过分数函数的法切分解，证明了用无条件切向分数替代条件切向分数的误差上界由条件互信息决定，并提出了一种投影-朗之万初始化加引导反向去噪的方法，在实验中优于强投影基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于投影的采样器通过校正观测分量来强制执行测量一致性，但测量一致性本身无法决定可行集上的概率质量分布，可能导致有偏的条件样本。本文旨在分析并解决这一问题。

Method: 通过分数函数的法切（normal-tangent）分解分析偏差来源；在加性高斯噪声下，观测方向分数由测量精确决定，仅切向条件分数未知；证明用无条件切向分数替代的误差上界由维度无关的条件互信息给出；提出投影-朗之万初始化加引导反向去噪的方法。

Result: 将误差分解为初始化误差和路径分数失配误差，后者上界由条件互信息控制；提出的方法在图像修复和超分辨率实验中优于强投影基线。

Conclusion: 理论分析揭示了投影式条件扩散采样的偏差根源，信息论分解为设计更好的初始化与引导策略提供了依据，所提方法有效提升了条件采样质量。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [16] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列和边选择两个阶段，通过预编码边聚合方案、非自回归架构和分层强化学习，有效解决多图车辆路径问题中的可扩展性问题，在六个VRP变体上以更快的训练和推理速度达到或超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法大多局限于欧几里得设置或简单图，而多图中并行边代表不同旅行选项（如距离与时间权衡），现有方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略拆分为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案、非自回归架构用于边阶段，以及分层强化学习方法联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解质量上匹配或超越SOTA，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF通过策略分解有效缓解了多图VRP的可扩展性问题，为处理更复杂的图结构路由问题提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [17] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两类互补的梯度计算策略（通过模拟的自动微分法和显式离散伴随法），用于求解含状态事件半隐式微分代数方程的参数优化问题，重点解决了代数变量隐式定义、事件时间依赖参数以及重置映射不连续等核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含状态事件的微分代数方程（DAEs）广泛存在于受代数方程约束且存在模式切换、冲击或状态重初始化的动力学系统中。对此类系统进行基于梯度的参数学习面临三大困难：代数变量隐式定义、事件时间随参数变化、重置映射引入不连续性。现有方法缺乏针对此类问题的系统化可微优化框架。

Method: 将学习问题形式化为带DAE动力学、代数约束、守卫方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题。提出两种互补梯度计算方法：(1) 通过模拟的自动微分法——在向量场内求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理对代数求解过程求导，通过分段可微积分处理事件；(2) 显式离散伴随法——将前向模拟表示为事件分割的残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度，明确将伴随法中的残差项视为等式约束而非启发式惩罚项。

Result: 两种方法均能为前向模拟所选的事件路径提供梯度，在固定事件顺序和横截守卫穿越条件下有效。从梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性四个维度对两种方法进行了系统比较。

Conclusion: 本研究为含事件半隐式DAE的可微参数优化提供了完整框架，阐明了伴随方法中残差项的约束本质，为后续在机器人、多体动力学等领域的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [18] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WARDEN分布鲁棒对抗训练框架，通过f-散度模糊集动态重加权对抗样本，在KL散度下将目标简化为log-sum-exp形式，显著降低攻击成功率且计算成本与现有方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对抗训练方法计算昂贵且难以扩展，而连续对抗训练方法（如CAT、CAPO）虽更高效，但缺乏对困难样本的自动侧重机制，需要更鲁棒的训练范式。

Method: 基于分布鲁棒优化，在经验训练分布的f-散度球内优化最坏情况对抗损失，通过凸对偶形式将目标简化为KL散度下的log-sum-exp形式，利用动态参数控制重加权强度。

Result: 在多种LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低攻击成功率，计算开销和模型效用与CAT、CAPO、MixAT基线相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN提供了一种实用且可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法，通过信息论目标实现了效率与安全性的良好平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成压缩感知使用预训练生成器的值域作为非线性模型来恢复结构化信号，但未考虑条件生成模型（如提示条件）在采样和恢复中的不同作用。

Result: 提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持与近最优生成压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数；提示不匹配会产生显式的兼容性惩罚；Stable Diffusion实验表明提示能有效重塑Christoffel采样分布并影响图像恢复。

Conclusion: 提示应被视为对感知、近似和恢复具有不同影响的设计变量。

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

</details>

*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理会导致模型崩溃（始终预测"Yes"或"No"），本文提出基于图的语义损失函数和动态lambda调度，在Gemma 270M上将准确率从崩溃状态的73.9%提升至70.4%（传递性）和68.6%（d-separation），实现稳定的上下文相关预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理任务时会导致灾难性的模型崩溃，模型学习到"总是回答Yes/No"的平凡解，虽然准确率看似很高（73.9%），但实际上没有学到任何因果推理能力。

Method: 提出语义损失函数（Semantic Loss），包含基于图的逻辑约束和动态lambda调度（Dynamic Lambda Scheduling），防止模型学习到平凡解。

Result: 在传递性任务上达到70.4%准确率，在d-separation任务上达到68.6%准确率，相比崩溃基线提升42.7%。对抗性评估中语义模型达67-70%准确率，崩溃模型仅43-71%。在20万+样本和5个模型变体上验证。

Conclusion: 语义损失对于Transformer实现稳定的因果推理是必要且不可或缺的，标准微调必然导致模型崩溃，而基于图的逻辑约束可以有效防止这一现象。

Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

</details>


### [21] [Robustness of Graph Self-Supervised Learning to Real-World Noise: A Case Study on Text-Driven Biomedical Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05463)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次全面评估了图自监督学习(GSSL)在文本驱动噪声图上的表现，提出了NATD-GSSL统一框架，发现关系重建对噪声敏感而特征重建更鲁棒，对比学习受噪声影响较小但依赖下游任务对齐，双向关系消息传递架构更适合噪声图，在无监督术语分类任务上相比预训练语言模型基线提升7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GSSL方法假设图是干净且人工整理的，但NLP技术的进步使得从文本中自动提取知识图谱成为可能，这一过程引入的噪声在以往鲁棒性研究中未被充分探索（先前研究仅依赖合成扰动）。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估GSSL方法在真实噪声文本驱动图上的表现。

Method: 提出NATD-GSSL统一框架，包含自动图构建、图细化和GSSL三个模块。采用双图评估协议：从MedMentions构建噪声图，与干净的UMLS参考图进行对比，通过共享的金标准对齐。系统比较不同预训练任务（关系重建、特征重建、对比学习）和GNN架构（双向关系消息传递、单向关系消息传递）在噪声环境下的鲁棒性。

Result: 关系重建对噪声高度敏感，需要良好定义的图模式；特征重建最为鲁棒，性能接近干净图设置；对比学习受噪声影响较小，但依赖与下游任务的对齐程度。双向关系消息传递GNN更适合噪声文本驱动图，而单向关系消息传递架构在干净图上表现最优。整体相比预训练语言模型基线提升7%。

Conclusion: NATD-GSSL为在真实世界噪声图上应用GSSL提供了实用指导，揭示了不同预训练任务和GNN架构对噪声的鲁棒性差异，为未来文本驱动图上的自监督学习研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) offers a powerful paradigm for learning graph representations without labeled data. However, existing work assumes clean, manually curated graphs. Recent advances in NLP enable the large-scale automatic extraction of knowledge graphs from text, opening new opportunities for GSSL while introducing substantial real-world noise. This type of noise remains largely unexplored, as prior robustness studies typically rely on synthetic perturbations. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of GSSL methods on text-driven graphs for unsupervised term typing. We introduce Noise-Aware Text-Driven Graph GSSL (NATD-GSSL), a unified framework that combines automatic graph construction, graph refinement, and GSSL. Our evaluation follows a dual-graph protocol that contrasts a noisy graph derived from MedMentions with a clean Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) reference graph, aligned through a shared gold standard. Our results reveal variability in robustness across both pretext tasks and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures. Relation reconstruction is highly sensitive to noise and benefits from well-defined schemas, whereas feature reconstruction is considerably more robust, achieving performance comparable to clean-graph settings. Contrastive objectives are generally less affected by noise but depend strongly on alignment with downstream tasks. GNN architecture also plays a critical role: bidirectional relational message-passing designs are better suited to noisy, text-driven graphs, while unidirectional relational ones perform best on clean graphs. Overall, NATD-GSSL provides practical guidance for applying GSSL to real-world, noisy graphs and achieves up to a 7\% improvement over pretrained language model baselines. All code and benchmarks are publicly available at https://github.com/OthmaneKabal/MC2GAE.

</details>


### [22] [Approximate Next Policy Sampling: Replacing Conservative Target Policy Updates in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05481)
*Dillon Sandhu,Ronald Parr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近似下一策略采样（ANPS）方法，通过修改训练数据分布而非约束策略更新来解决强化学习中的"鸡生蛋"问题。基于ANPS，作者设计了稳定值近似策略迭代（SV-API）算法，并将其应用于PPO得到SV-PPO，在Atari和连续控制基准测试中匹配或提升了性能，同时实现了更大的目标策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中存在一个经典的"鸡生蛋"问题：要安全地改进策略，价值函数必须在更新后策略的状态访问分布上保持准确，但该分布未知且无法采样用于训练价值函数。传统的保守更新方法虽能解决此问题，但代价是缩小策略更新幅度。

Method: 提出ANPS方法，通过修改训练数据分布（而非约束策略更新）来解决该问题。具体实现为SV-API算法：在标准近似策略迭代循环中，固定目标策略不变，由迭代更新的行为策略收集相关经验，仅在满足收敛准则后才承诺新策略。将SV-API应用于PPO得到SV-PPO。

Result: SV-PPO在高维离散（Atari）和连续控制基准测试中匹配或提升了PPO的性能，同时能够执行大幅更大的目标策略更新。

Conclusion: ANPS为解决RL中的经典挑战提供了一种可行的新方案，通过修改训练分布而非约束更新幅度，实现了更大策略更新的安全改进。

Abstract: We revisit a classic "chicken-and-egg" problem in reinforcement learning: to safely improve a policy, the value function must be accurate on the state-visitation distribution of the updated policy. That distribution over states is unknown and cannot be sampled for the purposes of training the value function. Conservative updates solve this problem, but at the cost of shrinking the policy update. This paper explores an alternative solution, Approximate Next Policy Sampling (ANPS), which addresses the problem by modifying the training distribution rather than constraining the policy update. ANPS is satisfied if the distribution of the training data approximates that of the next policy. To demonstrate the feasibility and efficacy of ANPS, we introduce Stable Value Approximate Policy Iteration (SV-API). SV-API modifies the standard approximate policy iteration loop to hold the target policy fixed while an iteratively updated behavioral policy gathers relevant experience. It only commits to a new policy once a convergence criterion has been met. If certain stability criteria are met, the update is guaranteed to be safe; otherwise, it remains no less safe than standard approximate policy iteration. Applying SV-API to PPO yields Stable Value PPO (SV-PPO), which matches or improves performance on high-dimensional discrete (Atari) and continuous control benchmarks while executing substantially larger target policy updates. These results demonstrate the viability of ANPS as a new solution to this classic challenge in RL.

</details>


### [23] [MEMOA: Massive Mixtures of Online Agents via Mean-Field Decentralized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05492)
*Xuwei Yang,David B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化的联邦学习最优策略，通过最小化最弱客户端的遗憾（worst-client regret）得到闭式解，并证明在大规模群体极限下该策略渐近收敛于不可扩展的纳什最优集中式策略，同时利用在线加权机制优化混合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在规模化时面临计算与通信开销瓶颈，而去中心化策略中每个智能体仅依赖自身状态和均值场摘要。现有方法缺乏最优性保证，本文旨在推导去中心化策略的闭式最优解并建立与集中式策略的理论联系。

Method: 1) 基于最坏客户端/极小极大准则（minimax criterion）推导去中心化策略的闭式最优解；2) 证明大群体极限下策略收敛于纳什最优集中式策略；3) 引入在线加权机制优化服务器计算的客户端预测混合，进一步提升均值预测性能。

Result: 1) 得到了唯一最优去中心化策略的闭式解；2) 证明了该策略在大规模群体极限下渐近收敛于纳什最优集中式策略；3) 在线加权机制改善了均值预测；4) 数值实验验证了理论保证，且去中心化策略优于自然贪心基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的去中心化最优策略在理论最优性、可扩展性与经验性能方面均具有优势，为大规模联邦学习中的去中心化智能体协作提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: In the modern age of large-scale AI, federated learning has become an increasingly important tool for training large populations of AI agents; however, its computational and communication costs can rapidly fail to scale with the number of agents. This is precisely where decentralized agentic strategies shine: each agent acts autonomously, using only its own state together with a minimal summary of the ensemble, namely the mean-field. We derive the unique optimal decentralized policy in closed form. Optimality is characterized through a worst-client/minimax criterion: minimizing the under-performer regret, namely the maximal online cost incurred by the weakest agent in the ensemble. We further prove that the resulting decentralized policy asymptotically converges, in the large-population limit, to the Nash-optimal centralized policy, whose direct computation is not scalable. We use an online weighting mechanism to optimize the server-computed mixture of client predictions, thereby improving the mean prediction in addition to the previously optimized weakest-client prediction. Numerical experiments verify our theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that our decentralized policy typically outperforms natural greedy decentralized baselines.

</details>


### [24] [Shortcut Solutions Learned by Transformers Impair Continual Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05495)
*William T. Redman,Erik C. Johnson,Brian Robinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在持续学习（CL）场景下扩展了LEGO框架（称为“continual LEGO”），系统研究了前馈（BERT）和循环（ALBERT）Transformer模型在利用已有表征学习新相关经验方面的能力。发现BERT学到捷径解，限制了泛化和正向迁移；而ALBERT学到类似For循环的解，表现出更好的CL性能。但在需要跨经验组合的CL任务中，两类模型均失败。ALBERT可通过跨经验数据混合训练恢复性能，但BERT因捷径解固化而无法恢复。结果表明循环架构ALBERT具有更适合CL的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补先前研究空白：虽然Transformer的组合推理能力已被广泛研究，但其如何利用已有表征来学习新的相关经验（即持续学习能力）尚缺乏系统理解。

Method: 将LEGO框架扩展到持续学习设置（continual LEGO），系统比较前馈Transformer（BERT）和循环Transformer（ALBERT）在CL场景下的表现，分析其学习到的计算解决方案。

Result: BERT学到捷径解，限制泛化和正向迁移；ALBERT学到类似For循环的解，CL性能更优。在需要跨经验组合的CL任务中两类模型均失败。ALBERT可通过混合数据训练恢复性能，BERT则因捷径解固化而无法恢复。

Conclusion: 循环架构ALBERT可能具有更适合持续学习的归纳偏置，为未来研究Transformer架构与涌现计算解决方案之间的相互作用提供了动机。

Abstract: Identifying and exploiting common features across domains is at the heart of the human ability to make analogies, and is believed to be crucial for the ability to continually learn. To do this successfully, general and flexible computational strategies must be developed. While the extent to which Transformer neural network models can perform compositional reasoning has been the subject of intensive recent investigation, little work has been done to systematically understand how well these models can leverage their representations to learn new, related experiences. To address this gap, we expand the previously developed Learning Equality and Group Operations (LEGO) framework to a continual learning (CL) setting ("continual LEGO"). Using this continual LEGO experimental paradigm, we study the capability of feedforward and recurrent Transformer models to perform CL. We find that BERT, a canonical feedforward Transformer model, learns shortcut solutions that limits its ability to generalize and prevents strong forward transfer to new experiences. In contrast, we find evidence supporting the hypothesis that ALBERT, a recurrent version of BERT, learns a For loop-esque solution, which leads to better CL performance. When applying BERT and ALBERT models to a CL setting that requires composition across experiences, we find that both model families fail. Our investigation suggests that ALBERT models can have their performance drop rescued by use of training strategies that combine data across experiences, but this is not true for BERT models, where a detrimental shortcut solution becomes entrenched with initial training. Our results demonstrate that the recurrent ALBERT model may have an inductive bias better suited for CL and motivate future investigation of the interplay between Transformer architecture and computational solutions that emerge in modern models and tasks.

</details>


### [25] [Online Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05497)
*Yuheng Lai,Garvesh Raskutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线局部共形预测（OLCP）及其变体OLCP-Hedge，通过结合在线自适应与协变量依赖的局部化方法，在非交换数据的在线学习与时间序列场景下实现长期有效的覆盖率，并生成更窄的预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线共形方法（如ACI）虽然能实现长期有效性，但依赖全局校准，在面对协变量异质性时效率低下，无法充分反映数据中的 heterogeneity。

Method: 提出OLCP，将在线自适应与协变量依赖的局部化相结合；进一步提出OLCP-Hedge，将带宽选择视为在线专家聚合问题，通过约束在线凸优化框架实现，并提供两种算法的覆盖保证。

Result: 模拟与真实数据实验表明，所提方法在实现有效长期覆盖率的同时，预测集比现有基线更窄。

Conclusion: OLCP及其变体在非交换数据场景下兼顾了覆盖有效性与预测效率，优于现有全局校准方法。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification for general models with exchangeable data. However, in the online learning and time-series settings, exchangeability is not satisfied. Existing online conformal methods, such as adaptive conformal inference (ACI), can achieve long-run validity, yet they remain inefficient under covariate heterogeneity because they rely on global calibration. We propose \emph{Online Localized Conformal Prediction (OLCP)}, which combines online adaptation with covariate-dependent localization to better reflect heterogeneity. To reduce sensitivity to the localization bandwidth, we further develop \emph{OLCP-Hedge}, which performs bandwidth selection as an online expert aggregation problem using a constrained online convex optimization framework. Importantly, we provide coverage guarantees for both algorithms and demonstrate through simulations and real-data experiments that the proposed methods attain valid long-run coverage with narrower prediction sets than existing baselines.

</details>


### [26] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非近视路径策略梯度（NM-PPG）方法，通过连续松弛和直通 rollout 实现非近视特征获取策略的端到端优化，在合成和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动特征获取（AFA）中特征获取有成本，需自适应决定获取哪些特征及何时停止预测。现有方法存在方差高或近视策略等问题。

Method: 将AFA建模为POMDP；引入连续松弛使路径梯度可通过完整获取轨迹传播；提出直通rollout方案（前向硬获取，反向软松弛）；使用熵正则化和分段温度锐化稳定优化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，NM-PPG性能优于当前最先进的AFA基线方法。

Conclusion: 非近视路径策略梯度方法有效解决了AFA中的决策优化问题，通过连续松弛和直通梯度实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy. To better align training with deployment, we further develop a straight-through rollout scheme that follows hard feature acquisitions in the forward pass while backpropagating through the corresponding soft relaxation in the backward pass. We stabilize optimization with entropy regularization and staged temperature sharpening. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that NM-PPG yields superior performance relative to state-of-the-art AFA baselines.

</details>


### [27] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpenG2G是一个用于AI数据中心与电网运行时协调的仿真平台，支持多种控制范式，通过模块化架构（真实AI服务测量驱动的数据中心后端、高保真电网模拟器、通用控制器接口）帮助评估数据中心灵活性对电网的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI算力需求激增和新数据中心建设给电网带来容量和可靠性挑战，导致数据中心并网延迟长达数年，制约AI发展。为缓解这一压力，需要理解大型数据中心对电网的影响并设计有效的协调策略。

Method: 构建OpenG2G仿真平台，采用模块化可扩展架构：1）数据中心后端基于生产级AI服务的真实测量数据；2）电网后端基于高保真电网模拟器；3）通用控制器接口闭环连接两者。支持经典控制、优化控制、基于学习的控制器等多种控制范式的实现与对比。

Result: OpenG2G能够回答广泛的协调问题，允许用户实现和对比不同控制范式，量化AI模型和部署选择对数据中心灵活性和协调结果的影响，并通过真实电网场景和AI工作负载验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: OpenG2G为AI数据中心与电网的运行时协调提供了有效的仿真工具，有助于缓解AI发展中的电网瓶颈问题。

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>

*Badr Moufad,Albina Ilina,Hai Victor Habi,Salem Lahlou,Yazid Janati,Hagit Messer,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将商用微波链路（CML）降雨场重建建模为贝叶斯逆问题，以扩散模型作为高保真空间先验，通过多种无训练后验采样方法实现降雨估计，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CML降雨重建方法将CML简化为点传感器，忽略了信号衰减与降雨之间的线积分关系，在非均匀降水条件下性能退化严重，需要更精确的建模方法。

Method: 将降雨场重建视为以扩散模型为先验的贝叶斯逆问题，利用Plug-and-Play、序贯蒙特卡洛和副本交换等多种无训练后验采样方法进行降雨估计。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法相比现有CML重建基线取得了一致且显著的性能提升，并更好地保留了关键降雨统计量。

Conclusion: 扩散模型作为空间先验在CML降雨重建中优于截断高斯过程，贝叶斯逆问题框架为训练后验采样提供了灵活有效的手段。

Abstract: Commercial Microwave Links (CMLs) offer dense spatial coverage for rainfall sensing but produce path-integrated measurements that make accurate ground-level reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically oversimplify CMLs as point sensors and neglect line integration relating rainfall to signal attenuation, resulting in degraded performance under heterogeneous precipitation. In this work, we view rain field reconstruction as a Bayesian inverse problem with Diffusion Models (DMs) as high-fidelity spatial priors. We show that diffusion models better preserve key rainfall statistics compared to censored Gaussian processes. Framing rainfall estimation as a Bayesian inverse problem with a DM prior enables training-free posterior sampling using a broad family of methods, including Plug-and-Play, Sequential Monte Carlo, and Replica Exchange methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over established CML-based reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [29] [MOSAIC: Module Discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal Learning for Scientific Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05524)
*Shicheng Fan,Nour Elhendawy,Jianle Sun,Ke Fang,Kun Zhang,Yihang Wang,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC提出了一种稀疏时序VAE方法，将时序CRL的可识别性与观测变量的支持恢复相结合，通过加性解码器为每个潜在变量恢复一组稀疏关联的观测变量，从而实现模块级可解释性，适用于科学时间序列中的潜在机制发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果表示学习（CRL）虽然提供了可识别性保证，但可识别性并不等同于可解释性：潜在语义通常事后通过与已知ground-truth因子的对齐来指定。这在科学时间序列中尤为受限，因为底层机制未知，而发现可解释的结构是主要目标。相反，科学观测（如残基对距离、气候指数或过程传感器）天然具有语义，对应命名的物理量。因此，关键问题在于：能否将观测的可解释性转移到可识别的潜在空间？

Method: 提出MOSAIC（Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning），一种稀疏时序VAE模型。它通过基于状态的时序变化识别潜在变量，并使用加性解码器为每个潜在变量恢复一组稀疏的关联观测变量，实现模块级可解释性。该方法利用ANOVA主效应支持在一般光滑混合函数下的可识别性，并为可处理的稀疏加性变体提供了有限样本恢复保证。

Result: 在RNA分子动力学、太阳风、ENSO气候、田纳西-伊士曼过程以及合成托卡马克基准等多个科学时间序列数据集上，MOSAIC恢复了领域一致的变量分组，实现了对潜在机制的可解释发现。

Conclusion: MOSAIC成功地将观测变量的语义可解释性转移到可识别的潜在空间，通过在加性解码器中引入稀疏性，实现了模块级别的可解释性，为科学时间序列中潜在因果机制的发现提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) seeks to recover latent variables with identifiability guarantees, typically up to permutation and component-wise reparameterization under appropriate assumptions. However, identifiability does not imply interpretability: latent semantics are typically assigned post hoc by alignment with known ground-truth factors. This limitation is particularly acute in scientific time series, where underlying mechanisms are unknown and discovering interpretable structure is a primary goal. In contrast, scientific observations (such as residue-pair distances, climate indices, or process sensors) are inherently semantic, as they correspond to named physical quantities. This raises a key question: can the interpretability of observations be transferred to the identifiable latent space? We propose MOSAIC (Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning), a sparse temporal VAE that integrates temporal CRL identifiability with support recovery over observed variables. MOSAIC identifies latent variables via regime-conditioned temporal variation, and recovers for each latent a sparse set of associated observations through an additive decoder, yielding module-level interpretability. We show that ANOVA main-effect supports are identifiable under general smooth mixing functions, and provide finite-sample recovery guarantees for a tractable sparse-additive variant. Empirically, MOSAIC recovers domain-consistent variable groups across RNA molecular dynamics, solar wind, ENSO climate, the Tennessee Eastman process, and a synthetic tokamak benchmark, enabling interpretable discovery of latent mechanisms in scientific time series.

</details>


### [30] [Energy Generative Modeling: A Lyapunov-based Energy Matching Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05530)
*Yixuan Wang,Wenqian Xue,Warren E. Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于静态标量能量函数的生成模型统一在Wasserstein空间上的密度传输框架中，将训练和采样视为同一非线性控制问题的两种初始条件实例，并利用KL散度作为Lyapunov函数，推导出Langevin采样的有限步停止准则，证明了确定性梯度流不存在Lyapunov证书，同时展示了训练能量函数的加性组合仍保持显式Gibbs不变测度和闭环Lyapunov证书，实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于静态标量能量函数的生成模型将训练和采样视为分离的过程，缺乏统一的数学框架；同时，Langevin采样缺乏有限步停止准则，确定性梯度流缺少收敛保证。本文旨在通过非线性控制理论统一训练与采样，并解决这些理论空白。

Method: 将训练和采样重新形式化为Wasserstein空间上的密度传输问题，建模为以KL散度作为Lyapunov函数的非线性控制问题；训练和采样被视为同一主动力学的两种不同初始条件实例。基于此框架，推导Langevin采样的有限步停止准则，并证明确定性梯度流无Lyapunov证书；同时证明训练能量函数的加性组合保留Gibbs不变测度和Lyapunov证书。

Result: 推导出Langevin采样的有限步停止准则；证明确定性梯度流不存在Lyapunov证书；证明训练能量函数的加性组合保持显式Gibbs不变测度和闭环Lyapunov证书；在合成分布上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该统一框架将静态标量能量生成模型与非线性控制理论工具箱连接，为受约束生成（如障碍函数）和加速采样（如收缩度量）开辟了新方向，弥补了训练与采样之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: Generative models based on static scalar energy functions represent an emerging paradigm in which a single time independent potential drives sample generation through its gradient field, eliminating the need for time conditioning entirely. We unify the training and sampling phases of this paradigm, conventionally treated as separate procedures, within a single framework: density transport on the Wasserstein space, cast as a nonlinear control problem in which the Kullback Leibler (KL) divergence serves as a Lyapunov function. Training and sampling are then two instances of this same master dynamics, differing only in initial condition. Within this autonomous framework we develop two analytic results. First, since the Lyapunov certificate is asymptotic, we derive a finite step stopping criterion for Langevin sampling and prove that no Lyapunov certificate exists for the deterministic gradient flow on the same energy landscape. Second, the reformulation brings the toolkit of nonlinear control theory to bear on static scalar energy generative modeling, that is, we show that additive composition of trained scalar energies retains an explicit Gibbs invariant measure and inherits the closed-loop Lyapunov certificate. Beyond these immediate results, this reformulation bridges static scalar energy generative models with the full toolkit of nonlinear control theory, opening the door to barrier functions for constrained generation and contraction metrics for accelerated sampling. Experiments on synthetic distributions validate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [31] [Adaptive Q-Chunking for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05544)
*Nandiraju Gireesh,Yuanliang Ju,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应Q分块(AQC)方法，通过在每状态比较不同分块大小的优势值(经折扣因子归一化)来选择最优动作分块大小，解决固定分块导致的次优问题，在OGBench和Robomimic上达到SOTA，并提升VLA模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线到在线强化学习中的动作分块方法在所有状态下使用固定分块大小，这在接触事件附近（需要短分块实现反应控制）和自由空间运动（需要长分块实现更好的信用分配）时是次优的。

Method: 提出AQC方法：1) 训练多个不同分块大小的critic；2) 计算每个分块大小相对于每水平基线的优势值；3) 通过折扣因子归一化解决折扣尺度不匹配问题；4) 选择归一化优势最大的分块大小作为动作输出。

Result: 在OGBench和Robomimic上达到SOTA离线与在线成功率；在RoboCasa-GR1任务上显著提升VLA模型性能；理论证明了优势选择器的噪声鲁棒性边界以及自适应分块对任何固定分块的价值优势。

Conclusion: AQC通过自适应选择分块大小解决了固定分块策略的次优问题，其优势比较准则在无真实信号时给出无偏近随机选择，在有信号时具备判别能力，且可推广到大规模VLA模型。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning with action chunking eliminates multi-step off-policy bias and enables temporally coherent exploration, but all existing methods use a fixed chunk size across every state. This is suboptimal: near contact events the agent needs short chunks for reactive control, while during free-space motion long chunks provide better credit assignment. The natural solution is to train critics for several chunk sizes and select the best one at each state, but naive comparison of learned critic values systematically collapses to the shortest chunk due to discount-scale mismatch, and degrades to noise in low-value states. We propose Adaptive Q-Chunking (AQC), which resolves both failures by comparing the advantage of each chunk size relative to a per-horizon baseline, normalized by the discount factor. This criterion converts biased wrong answers into unbiased near-random choices when no genuine signal exists, and becomes discriminative when a particular scale enables better planning. We prove theoretical bounds on the advantage selector's noise immunity and on the value dominance of adaptive chunking over any fixed chunk size. We demonstrate that AQC achieves state-of-the-art offline and online success rates on OGBench and Robomimic, and can be applied to enhance the performance of large-scale VLA models that predict action sequences, significantly boosting performance on RoboCasa-GR1 tasks.

</details>


### [32] [Accelerating LMO-Based Optimization via Implicit Gradient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05577)
*Won-Jun Jang,Si-Hyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LMO-IGT方法，利用隐式梯度传输（IGT）加速基于线性最小化预言机（LMO）的优化，在保持单梯度每次迭代结构的同时达到O(ε^{-3.5})的迭代复杂度，并引入统一框架和新的平稳性度量（正则化支撑函数）。实验表明Muon-IGT表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMO-based方法（如Lion、Muon）在经验上表现优异，但方差缩减技术计算开销大（需额外梯度评估），且理论框架在无约束和约束形式下碎片化，缺乏统一的理论理解。

Method: 提出LMO-IGT方法：在传输点处评估随机梯度，利用隐式梯度传输（IGT）加速收敛，同时保持每迭代仅需一次梯度计算的效率。引入正则化支撑函数（RSF）作为统一的平稳性度量，桥接梯度范数和Frank-Wolfe间隙的概念。

Result: 随机LMO达到O(ε^{-4})迭代复杂度；方差缩减LMO以额外梯度评估为代价达到O(ε^{-3})；LMO-IGT仅用单梯度每迭代即达到O(ε^{-3.5})。实验上LMO-IGT一致优于随机LMO基线，Muon-IGT在所有设置中性能最强。

Conclusion: LMO-IGT提供了一种有效且实用的加速机制，在计算开销可忽略的前提下显著提升了现代LMO-based优化方法的性能。

Abstract: Recent optimizers such as Lion and Muon have demonstrated strong empirical performance by normalizing gradient momentum via linear minimization oracles (LMOs). While variance reduction has been explored to accelerate LMO-based methods, it typically incurs substantial computational overhead due to additional gradient evaluations. At the same time, the theoretical understanding of LMO-based methods remains fragmented across unconstrained and constrained formulations. Motivated by these limitations, we propose \emph{LMO-IGT}, a new class of stochastic LMO-based methods leveraging implicit gradient transport (IGT). We further introduce a unified framework for stochastic LMO-based optimization together with a new stationarity measure, the \emph{regularized support function} (RSF), which bridges gradient-norm and Frank--Wolfe-gap notions within a common framework. By evaluating stochastic gradients at transported points, LMO-IGT accelerates convergence while retaining the single-gradient-per-iteration structure of standard stochastic LMO. Our analysis establishes that stochastic LMO achieves an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$, variance-reduced LMO achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ at the cost of additional gradient evaluations, and LMO-IGT achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3.5})$ using only a single stochastic gradient per iteration. Empirically, LMO-IGT consistently improves over stochastic LMO counterparts with negligible overhead. Among its instantiations, Muon-IGT achieves the strongest overall performance across evaluated settings, demonstrating that IGT provides an effective and practical acceleration mechanism for modern LMO-based optimization.

</details>


### [33] [When Can Voting Help, Hurt, or Change Course? Exact Structure of Binary Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05592)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多数投票在可交换重复正确性假设下的非单调行为，证明了投票曲线与有符号投票签名之间的等价性，并分析了可识别性、可实现性及估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定能力理论认为多数投票具有单调性（超过多数阈值时投票越多效果越好，低于时越差）。作者指出这一观点根本性地不完整，因为实际中重复正确性服从可交换性，存在潜在的正确性概率分布，导致投票曲线可能呈现非单调等复杂行为。

Method: 使用de Finetti表示定理刻画可交换重复正确性下的潜在分布；定义有符号投票签名（signed voting signature）记录每个二项方差尺度上高于和低于多数阈值的潜在质量差；证明投票曲线增量与签名之间的等价关系（增量是有符号Hausdorff矩，完整曲线唯一恢复签名）。

Result: 发现即使简单的潜在混合分布也能产生非单调投票曲线，甚至存在无限多次趋势变化的显式构造；证明了投票曲线与有符号投票签名之间的等价性（主定理）；揭示了形状现象、分支对称不可识别性、可实现性、变化性和端点速率；区分了两种估计情形：直接逐样本成功概率信息可恢复完整签名，而固定深度分组标签仅揭示有限前缀。

Conclusion: 多数投票行为由潜在分布完全决定但反之不唯一；签名视角统一解释了投票曲线的各种性质，并分离了不同信息条件下的估计能力。

Abstract: Majority voting is one of the few black-box interventions that can improve a fixed stochastic predictor: repeated access can be cheaper than changing a high-capability model. Classical fixed-competence theory makes this intervention look monotone -- more votes help above the majority threshold and hurt below it. We show that this picture is fundamentally incomplete. Under the de Finetti representation for exchangeable repeated correctness, voting is governed by a latent distribution of per-example correctness probabilities. Even simple latent mixtures can generate sharply different voting curves, including nonmonotone behavior and, in an explicit construction, infinitely many trend changes. The full latent law determines the curve, but the curve does not determine the law. The exact object recovered by voting is a signed voting signature: at each binomial variance scale, it records excess latent mass above rather than below the majority threshold. Our main theorem proves that the complete odd-budget curve and this signature are equivalent: the curve increments are signed Hausdorff moments, and the full curve recovers the signature uniquely. This viewpoint explains shape phenomena, branch-symmetric nonidentifiability, realizability, variation, and endpoint rates. It also separates estimation regimes: direct per-example success-probability information targets the full signature, whereas fixed-depth grouped labels reveal only a finite prefix.

</details>


### [34] [Optimal Contextual Pricing under Agnostic Non-Lipschitz Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05609)
*Jianyu Xu,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有线性估值和有界支撑不可知噪声的上下文动态定价问题，提出了Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$的最优遗憾界，匹配Kleinberg和Leighton (2003)的下界，并改进了Xu和Wang (2022)的$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在噪声分布不可知且非Lipschitz（存在任意跳跃和原子）的情况下，跨上下文插值论证失效，此前最优方法仅达到$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$遗憾界，存在遗憾间隙有待闭合。

Method: 提出Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，采用多项式时间的三步策略：随机化参数估计、保守残差网格探测、基于置信度的一步重定向。

Result: 算法实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$遗憾，匹配已知下界（对数因子内），改进了之前$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$的上界。

Conclusion: 在随机良态上下文下，该工作闭合了线性估值上下文定价在不可知非Lipschitz噪声分布下的长期开放遗憾间隙。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing with linear valuations and bounded-support agnostic noise, whose induced demand curve may be non-Lipschitz with arbitrary jumps and atoms. Such discontinuities break the cross-context interpolation arguments used by smooth-demand pricing algorithms, while the best previous method achieved only $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$ regret. We propose Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing, a polynomial-time algorithm that combines randomized parameter estimation, conservative residual-grid probing, and confidence-based one-step redirection. Our algorithm achieves $\tilde O(T^{2/3})$ optimal regret, matching the known lower bounds of Kleinberg and Leighton (2003) up to logarithmic factors and improving over the previous upper bound of Xu and Wang (2022). Under stochastic well-conditioned contexts, this closes the long-existing open regret gap in linear-valuation contextual pricing under agnostic non-Lipschitz noise distribution.

</details>


### [35] [LLMSpace: Carbon Footprint Modeling for Large Language Model Inference on LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05615)
*Lei Jiang,Adrian Ildefonso,Daniel Loveless,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMSpace是首个针对LEO卫星上LLM推理的碳足迹建模框架，联合考虑了运行碳、隐含碳、外围子系统、抗辐射硬件及LLM工作负载特性，揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计与运行寿命之间的关键权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理能耗快速增长引发能源与碳危机，太阳能AI卫星虽被提出用于缓解地面电力消耗，但其全生命周期碳足迹（含发射、制造及抗辐射硬件）尚未被充分理解。

Method: LLMSpace联合建模了运行碳与隐含碳、外围子系统、抗辐射加速器与内存，以及LLM特有的预填-解码行为和token生成等工作负载特征，使用真实卫星与GPU配置进行分析。

Result: 揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计与运行寿命之间的关键权衡关系。

Conclusion: 为可持续的太空LLM推理提供了首个系统级碳建模框架，有助于指导未来太空AI基础设施的绿色设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) impose rapidly growing energy demands, creating an emerging energy and carbon crisis driven by large-scale inference. Solar-powered, AI-enabled low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, but their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements. This paper presents \textit{LLMSpace}, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites. LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation. Using realistic satellite and GPU configurations, LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference. Source code: https://github.com/UnchartedRLab/LLMSpace.

</details>


### [36] [Region-adaptable retrieval of coastal biogeochemical parameters from near-surface hyperspectral remote sensing reflectance using physics-aware meta-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05623)
*Yiqing Guo,Nagur R. C. Cherukuru,Eric A. Lehmann,S. L. Kesav Unnithan,Tim J. Malthus,Gemma Kerrisk,Xiubin Qi,Faisal Islam,Tisham Dhar,Mark J. Doubell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段物理感知元学习框架，利用原位高光谱遥感反射率数据反演澳大利亚沿海水域的生物地球化学参数（如总悬浮物、溶解有机碳、叶绿素a）。第一阶段使用生物光学前向模型生成合成数据集预训练区域无关的基础模型，第二阶段用局部样本对模型进行微调。实验表明该方法优于五个基准模型，且预测的时间序列与实测数据在量级和动态变化上高度一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱原位传感在反演水生生物地球化学参数方面具有潜力，但不同水域间遥感反射率与BGC参数的关系因环境条件和生物光学特性差异而显著不同，导致算法泛化困难。

Method: 两阶段物理感知元学习框架：阶段一利用生物光学前向模型基于广泛的澳大利亚沿海水域原位光谱库生成大规模合成数据集，通过元学习预训练区域无关的基础模型；阶段二使用区域局部样本对预训练模型进行微调。

Result: (1) BGC参数及其高光谱Rrs特征在不同站点间存在明显区域差异；(2) 合成数据集在参数分布和参数间相关性方面与真实样本高度一致；(3) 所提方法在BGC反演中优于五个基准模型；(4) 实测与模型预测的BGC时间序列在量级和时变趋势上吻合良好。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段物理感知元学习框架能够有效克服跨水域BGC反演的区域泛化难题，实现高精度的沿海水质参数遥感反演。

Abstract: Hyperspectral in situ sensing has shown promise in retrieving aquatic biogeochemical (BGC) parameters, such as total suspended solids, dissolved organic carbon, and total chlorophyll-a, for cost-effective monitoring of coastal water quality. However, generalising such retrieval algorithms across water bodies remains challenging, as the relationship between remote sensing reflectance (Rrs) and BGC parameters can vary considerably from one region to another due to regional distinctions in environmental conditions and biogeochemistry that lead to different BGC ranges and bio-optical properties. In this study, we propose a two-stage physics-aware meta-learning framework for retrieving coastal BGC parameters from near-surface Rrs observations. In the first stage, a bio-optical forward model is used to generate a large synthetic dataset based on an in situ bio-optical spectral library with broad representativeness of Australian coastal waters. This dataset is then used to pretrain a region-agnostic base model with meta-learning, allowing the model to learn fundamental physical relationships. In the second stage, the pretrained base model is fine-tuned for specific regions with local samples. We collected in situ hyperspectral Rrs and BGC measurements from five geographically distinct sites in Australian coastal waters. Our experimental results suggest: (1) the BGC parameters and their corresponding hyperspectral Rrs signatures exhibited clear regional distinctions among the experimental sites; (2) the synthetic dataset was physically plausible and closely aligned with real-world samples in both parameter distributions and inter-parameter correlations; (3) the proposed approach outperformed five benchmark models in BGC retrieval; and (4) time series of in situ measured and model-predicted BGC parameters showed good agreement in both magnitude and temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [37] [Information-Preserving Domain Transfer with Unlabeled Data in Misspecified Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05652)
*Joon Jang,Eunho Jeong,Kyu Sung Choi,Hyeonjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIN是一个基于仿真的推断框架，通过无标签、无配对的真实观测数据，在领域迁移中保留与参数相关的互信息，从而在模型错误指定下提升后验推断的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于仿真的推断方法在模型错误指定下可靠性下降，而传统方法仅实现边际分布对齐，未能保留后验推断所需的参数相关信息。

Method: SPIN框架在训练时将有标签的仿真观测数据翻译到真实域再翻译回仿真域，利用原始标签鼓励保留参数相关互信息的领域迁移；测试时通过学到的真实到仿真映射将真实观测转换到仿真域进行后验推断。

Result: 在受控合成和物理真实基准上，SPIN改善了真实世界的后验推断效果，且错误指定越严重，提升越明显。

Conclusion: SPIN通过保留参数相关信息的领域转移，有效减轻了模型错误指定对基于仿真的推断的影响，在无需真实参数标签或配对数据的情况下实现鲁棒的后验推断。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) provides amortized Bayesian parameter inference from simulator-generated data without requiring explicit likelihood evaluation. Its reliability can degrade under model misspecification, where real-world observations are not well represented by the simulator used for training. Existing methods using unlabeled real-world data often align simulated and real-world data distributions, but marginal alignment alone does not directly preserve parameter-relevant information needed for posterior inference. We propose SPIN, an SBI framework with parameter-relevant information-preserving domain transfer using unlabeled, unpaired real-world observations. During training, SPIN translates labeled simulator observations toward the real-world domain and back to the simulator domain, using the original simulator labels to encourage domain transfer that preserves parameter-relevant mutual information. At test time, the learned real-to-simulator transport maps real-world observations into the simulator domain for posterior inference, without requiring real-world parameter labels or paired real--simulator observations. Across controlled synthetic and physical real-world benchmarks, SPIN improves real-world posterior inference, with the improvement becoming clearer as misspecification increases.

</details>


### [38] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05660)
*Yufeng Yang,Fangning Zhuo,Ziyi Chen,Heng Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布鲁棒多目标优化（DR-MOO）框架，将分布偏移纳入多目标优化，并设计了双循环和单循环两种多梯度下降算法，分别实现O(ε^{-12})和O(ε^{-4})的样本复杂度，达到ε-Pareto驻点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法未考虑数据分布偏移（distributional shift），而在实际应用中分布偏移普遍存在，亟需一种鲁棒的MOO框架。

Method: 提出DR-MOO框架，定义Pareto-type解概念；利用Lagrangian对偶重构，设计双循环MGDA（内环估计对偶变量）；进一步引入梯度裁剪处理广义光滑和有偏梯度估计，提出单循环双裁剪MGDA。

Result: 双循环MGDA样本复杂度O(ε^{-12})，单循环双裁剪MGDA显著提升至O(ε^{-4})；实验表明与SOTA MGDA基线方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: DR-MOO有效解决了MOO中的分布偏移问题，单循环算法在理论效率上大幅提升，且无需有界目标或梯度假设，适用于非凸场景。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has received growing attention in applications that require learning under multiple criteria. However, the existing MOO formulations do not explicitly account for distributional shifts in the data.
